I've uploaded a python code repository in the directory juanifioren__django-oidc-provider__0.7. Consider the following issue description:

<issue_description>
Handle optional scope on token and introspection endpoints for password and client_credentials grant_type
Parameter scope is defined as optional on authorization https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-4.1.1 and introspection https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc7662 requests. 

An enhancement is proposed to handle `scope` parameter on authorization requests with grant_type=password or grant_type= client_credentials (related to https://github.com/juanifioren/django-oidc-provider/pull/193):
1) if `scope` is not provided client's scope(s) are assigned to access token - 

- grant_type=client_credentials: current behavior of django-oidc-provider
- grant_type=password: currently django-oidc-provider assigns empty scope

2) if valid (supported by given client) scopes are requested requested scopes are assigned to access token
3) if requested scope(s) are not supported `raise TokenError('invalid_scope')`

An enhancement is proposed to display optional `scope` in introspection response controlled by new settings variable `OIDC_INTROSPECTION_RESPONSE_SCOPE_ENABLE`. 
</issue_description>

## Phase 1. READING: Understanding the Problem<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Let me carefully read and understand the issue description:

1. The issue is about handling optional `scope` parameter on token and introspection endpoints for password and client_credentials grant types.

2. According to RFC standards:
   - Parameter scope is defined as optional on authorization (RFC 6749 section 4.1.1) and introspection (RFC 7662) requests.

3. Current behavior issues:
   - For grant_type=client_credentials: current behavior assigns client's scope(s) to access token when scope is not provided (this is good)
   - For grant_type=password: currently assigns empty scope when scope is not provided (this needs to be fixed to assign client's scope(s))

4. Enhancement requirements:
   - If `scope` is not provided: assign client's scope(s) to access token for both grant types
   - If valid scopes are requested: assign requested scopes to access token  
   - If requested scopes are not supported: raise TokenError('invalid_scope')
   - Display optional `scope` in introspection response controlled by new settings variable `OIDC_INTROSPECTION_RESPONSE_SCOPE_ENABLE`

Key technical details:
- Methods/grant types involved: password, client_credentials
- Error to raise: TokenError('invalid_scope')
- New setting: OIDC_INTROSPECTION_RESPONSE_SCOPE_ENABLE
- Endpoints affected: token endpoint, introspection endpoint

Best practices to consider:
- Minimal changes to existing code
- Follow existing code patterns and conventions
- Proper error handling
- Configuration through settings
- Backward compatibility</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Your thought has been logged.</tool_response><|assistant|></think>### 1.1-1.3 Problem Analysis

The issue describes enhancements to handle optional `scope` parameter for OAuth 2.0 flows according to RFC standards:

**Key Technical Details:**
- **Grant Types**: `password` and `client_credentials`
- **Endpoints**: Token endpoint and introspection endpoint
- **Error**: `TokenError('invalid_scope')`
- **Setting**: `OIDC_INTROSPECTION_RESPONSE_SCOPE_ENABLE`
- **Methods**: Token request processing, introspection response generation

**Problem Statement:**
Currently, the django-oidc-provider handles scope differently for different grant types:
- `client_credentials`: Correctly assigns client's scopes when none provided
- `password`: Incorrectly assigns empty scope when none provided

The enhancement requires:
1. Consistent scope handling for both grant types when scope is not provided
2. Proper validation of requested scopes against client-supported scopes
3. Optional scope display in introspection responses via configuration

### 1.4 Steps to Reproduce
1. Make token requests with `grant_type=password` without providing `scope` parameter
2. Observe that empty scope is assigned instead of client's scopes
3. Make token requests with invalid scopes for either grant type
4. Check if proper `TokenError('invalid_scope')` is raised
5. Make introspection requests and check if scope is included in response based on setting

### 1.5 Best Practices
- Maintain backward compatibility
- Follow existing error handling patterns
- Use Django settings framework properly
- Minimal code changes
- Proper validation and error messaging

     9	
    10	OpenID Connect is a simple identity layer on top of the OAuth 2.0 protocol, which allows computing clients to verify the identity of an end-user based on the authentication performed by an authorization server, as well as to obtain basic profile information about the end-user in an interoperable and REST-like manner.      2	from base64 import urlsafe_b64encode
     3	import hashlib
     4	import logging
     5	from django.contrib.auth import authenticate
     6	
     7	from django.http import JsonResponse
     8	
     9	from oidc_provider.lib.errors import (
    10	    TokenError,
    11	    UserAuthError,
    12	)
    13	from oidc_provider.lib.utils.oauth2 import extract_client_auth
    14	from oidc_provider.lib.utils.token import (
    15	    create_id_token,
    16	    create_token,
    17	    encode_id_token,
    18	)
    19	from oidc_provider.models import (
    20	    Client,
    21	    Code,
    22	    Token,
    23	)
    24	from oidc_provider import settings
    25	
    26	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27	
    28	
    29	class TokenEndpoint(object):
    30	
    31	    def __init__(self, request):
    32	        self.request = request
    33	        self.params = {}
    34	        self.user = None
    35	        self._extract_params()
    36	
    37	    def _extract_params(self):
    38	        client_id, client_secret = extract_client_auth(self.request)
    39	
    40	        self.params['client_id'] = client_id
    41	        self.params['client_secret'] = client_secret
    42	        self.params['redirect_uri'] = self.request.POST.get('redirect_uri', '')
    43	        self.params['grant_type'] = self.request.POST.get('grant_type', '')
    44	        self.params['code'] = self.request.POST.get('code', '')
    45	        self.params['state'] = self.request.POST.get('state', '')
    46	        self.params['scope'] = self.request.POST.get('scope', '')
    47	        self.params['refresh_token'] = self.request.POST.get('refresh_token', '')
    48	        # PKCE parameter.
    49	        self.params['code_verifier'] = self.request.POST.get('code_verifier')
    50	
    51	        self.params['username'] = self.request.POST.get('username', '')
    52	        self.params['password'] = self.request.POST.get('password', '')
    53	
    54	    def validate_params(self):
    55	        try:
    56	            self.client = Client.objects.get(client_id=self.params['client_id'])
    57	        except Client.DoesNotExist:
    58	            logger.debug('[Token] Client does not exist: %s', self.params['client_id'])
    59	            raise TokenError('invalid_client')
    60	
    61	        if self.client.client_type == 'confidential':
    62	            if not (self.client.client_secret == self.params['client_secret']):
    63	                logger.debug('[Token] Invalid client secret: client %s do not have secret %s',
    64	                             self.client.client_id, self.client.client_secret)
    65	                raise TokenError('invalid_client')
    66	
    67	        if self.params['grant_type'] == 'authorization_code':
    68	            if not (self.params['redirect_uri'] in self.client.redirect_uris):
    69	                logger.debug('[Token] Invalid redirect uri: %s', self.params['redirect_uri'])
    70	                raise TokenError('invalid_client')
    71	
    72	            try:
    73	                self.code = Code.objects.get(code=self.params['code'])
    74	            except Code.DoesNotExist:
    75	                logger.debug('[Token] Code does not exist: %s', self.params['code'])
    76	                raise TokenError('invalid_grant')
    77	
    78	            if not (self.code.client == self.client) \
    79	               or self.code.has_expired():
    80	                logger.debug('[Token] Invalid code: invalid client or code has expired')
    81	                raise TokenError('invalid_grant')
    82	
    83	            # Validate PKCE parameters.
    84	            if self.params['code_verifier']:
    85	                if self.code.code_challenge_method == 'S256':
    86	                    new_code_challenge = urlsafe_b64encode(
    87	                            hashlib.sha256(self.params['code_verifier'].encode('ascii')).digest()
    88	                        ).decode('utf-8').replace('=', '')
    89	                else:
    90	                    new_code_challenge = self.params['code_verifier']
    91	
    92	                # TODO: We should explain the error.
    93	                if not (new_code_challenge == self.code.code_challenge):
    94	                    raise TokenError('invalid_grant')
    95	
    96	        elif self.params['grant_type'] == 'password':
    97	            if not settings.get('OIDC_GRANT_TYPE_PASSWORD_ENABLE'):
    98	                raise TokenError('unsupported_grant_type')
    99	
   100	            auth_args = (self.request,)
   101	            try:
   102	                inspect.getcallargs(authenticate, *auth_args)
   103	            except TypeError:
   104	                auth_args = ()
   105	
   106	            user = authenticate(
   107	                *auth_args,
   108	                username=self.params['username'],
   109	                password=self.params['password']
   110	            )
   111	
   112	            if not user:
   113	                raise UserAuthError()
   114	
   115	            self.user = user
   116	
   117	        elif self.params['grant_type'] == 'refresh_token':
   118	            if not self.params['refresh_token']:
   119	                logger.debug('[Token] Missing refresh token')
   120	                raise TokenError('invalid_grant')
   121	
   122	            try:
   123	                self.token = Token.objects.get(refresh_token=self.params['refresh_token'],
   124	                                               client=self.client)
   125	
   126	            except Token.DoesNotExist:
   127	                logger.debug(
   128	                    '[Token] Refresh token does not exist: %s', self.params['refresh_token'])
   129	                raise TokenError('invalid_grant')
   130	        elif self.params['grant_type'] == 'client_credentials':
   131	            if not self.client._scope:
   132	                logger.debug('[Token] Client using client credentials with empty scope')
   133	                raise TokenError('invalid_scope')
   134	        else:
   135	            logger.debug('[Token] Invalid grant type: %s', self.params['grant_type'])
   136	            raise TokenError('unsupported_grant_type')
   137	
   138	    def create_response_dic(self):
   139	        if self.params['grant_type'] == 'authorization_code':
   140	            return self.create_code_response_dic()
   141	        elif self.params['grant_type'] == 'refresh_token':
   142	            return self.create_refresh_response_dic()
   143	        elif self.params['grant_type'] == 'password':
   144	            return self.create_access_token_response_dic()
   145	        elif self.params['grant_type'] == 'client_credentials':
   146	            return self.create_client_credentials_response_dic()
   147	
   148	    def create_code_response_dic(self):
   149	        # See https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-4.1
   150	
   151	        token = create_token(
   152	            user=self.code.user,
   153	            client=self.code.client,
   154	            scope=self.code.scope)
   155	
   156	        if self.code.is_authentication:
   157	            id_token_dic = create_id_token(
   158	                user=self.code.user,
   159	                aud=self.client.client_id,
   160	                token=token,
   161	                nonce=self.code.nonce,
   162	                at_hash=token.at_hash,
   163	                request=self.request,
   164	                scope=token.scope,
   165	            )
   166	        else:
   167	            id_token_dic = {}
   168	        token.id_token = id_token_dic
   169	
   170	        # Store the token.
   171	        token.save()
   172	
   173	        # We don't need to store the code anymore.
   174	        self.code.delete()
   175	
   176	        dic = {
   177	            'access_token': token.access_token,
   178	            'refresh_token': token.refresh_token,
   179	            'token_type': 'bearer',
   180	            'expires_in': settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'),
   181	            'id_token': encode_id_token(id_token_dic, token.client),
   182	        }
   183	
   184	        return dic
   185	
   186	    def create_refresh_response_dic(self):
   187	        # See https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-6
   188	
   189	        scope_param = self.params['scope']
   190	        scope = (scope_param.split(' ') if scope_param else self.token.scope)
   191	        unauthorized_scopes = set(scope) - set(self.token.scope)
   192	        if unauthorized_scopes:
   193	            raise TokenError('invalid_scope')
   194	
   195	        token = create_token(
   196	            user=self.token.user,
   197	            client=self.token.client,
   198	            scope=scope)
   199	
   200	        # If the Token has an id_token it's an Authentication request.
   201	        if self.token.id_token:
   202	            id_token_dic = create_id_token(
   203	                user=self.token.user,
   204	                aud=self.client.client_id,
   205	                token=token,
   206	                nonce=None,
   207	                at_hash=token.at_hash,
   208	                request=self.request,
   209	                scope=token.scope,
   210	            )
   211	        else:
   212	            id_token_dic = {}
   213	        token.id_token = id_token_dic
   214	
   215	        # Store the token.
   216	        token.save()
   217	
   218	        # Forget the old token.
   219	        self.token.delete()
   220	
   221	        dic = {
   222	            'access_token': token.access_token,
   223	            'refresh_token': token.refresh_token,
   224	            'token_type': 'bearer',
   225	            'expires_in': settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'),
   226	            'id_token': encode_id_token(id_token_dic, self.token.client),
   227	        }
   228	
   229	        return dic
   230	
   231	    def create_access_token_response_dic(self):
   232	        # See https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-4.3
   233	
   234	        token = create_token(
   235	            self.user,
   236	            self.client,
   237	            self.params['scope'].split(' '))
   238	
   239	        id_token_dic = create_id_token(
   240	            token=token,
   241	            user=self.user,
   242	            aud=self.client.client_id,
   243	            nonce='self.code.nonce',
   244	            at_hash=token.at_hash,
   245	            request=self.request,
   246	            scope=token.scope,
   247	        )
   248	
   249	        token.id_token = id_token_dic
   250	        token.save()
   251	
   252	        return {
   253	            'access_token': token.access_token,
   254	            'refresh_token': token.refresh_token,
   255	            'expires_in': settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'),
   256	            'token_type': 'bearer',
   257	            'id_token': encode_id_token(id_token_dic, token.client),
   258	        }
   259	
   260	    def create_client_credentials_response_dic(self):
   261	        # See https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-4.4.3
   262	
   263	        token = create_token(
   264	            user=None,
   265	            client=self.client,
   266	            scope=self.client.scope)
   267	
   268	        token.save()
   269	
   270	        return {
   271	            'access_token': token.access_token,
   272	            'expires_in': settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'),
   273	            'token_type': 'bearer',
   274	            'scope': self.client._scope,
   275	        }
   276	
   277	    @classmethod
   278	    def response(cls, dic, status=200):
   279	        """
   280	        Create and return a response object.
   281	        """
   282	        response = JsonResponse(dic, status=status)
   283	        response['Cache-Control'] = 'no-store'
   284	        response['Pragma'] = 'no-cache'
   285	
   286	        return response
   287

     2	
     3	from django.http import JsonResponse
     4	
     5	from oidc_provider.lib.errors import TokenIntrospectionError
     6	from oidc_provider.lib.utils.common import run_processing_hook
     7	from oidc_provider.lib.utils.oauth2 import extract_client_auth
     8	from oidc_provider.models import Token, Client
     9	from oidc_provider import settings
    10	
    11	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    12	
    13	INTROSPECTION_SCOPE = 'token_introspection'
    14	
    15	
    16	class TokenIntrospectionEndpoint(object):
    17	
    18	    def __init__(self, request):
    19	        self.request = request
    20	        self.params = {}
    21	        self.token = None
    22	        self.id_token = None
    23	        self.client = None
    24	        self._extract_params()
    25	
    26	    def _extract_params(self):
    27	        # Introspection only supports POST requests
    28	        self.params['token'] = self.request.POST.get('token')
    29	        client_id, client_secret = extract_client_auth(self.request)
    30	        self.params['client_id'] = client_id
    31	        self.params['client_secret'] = client_secret
    32	
    33	    def validate_params(self):
    34	        if not (self.params['client_id'] and self.params['client_secret']):
    35	            logger.debug('[Introspection] No client credentials provided')
    36	            raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    37	        if not self.params['token']:
    38	            logger.debug('[Introspection] No token provided')
    39	            raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    40	        try:
    41	            self.token = Token.objects.get(access_token=self.params['token'])
    42	        except Token.DoesNotExist:
    43	            logger.debug('[Introspection] Token does not exist: %s', self.params['token'])
    44	            raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    45	        if self.token.has_expired():
    46	            logger.debug('[Introspection] Token is not valid: %s', self.params['token'])
    47	            raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    48	
    49	        try:
    50	            self.client = Client.objects.get(
    51	                client_id=self.params['client_id'],
    52	                client_secret=self.params['client_secret'])
    53	        except Client.DoesNotExist:
    54	            logger.debug('[Introspection] No valid client for id: %s',
    55	                         self.params['client_id'])
    56	            raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    57	        if INTROSPECTION_SCOPE not in self.client.scope:
    58	            logger.debug('[Introspection] Client %s does not have introspection scope',
    59	                         self.params['client_id'])
    60	            raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    61	
    62	        self.id_token = self.token.id_token
    63	
    64	        if settings.get('OIDC_INTROSPECTION_VALIDATE_AUDIENCE_SCOPE'):
    65	            if not self.token.id_token:
    66	                logger.debug('[Introspection] Token not an authentication token: %s',
    67	                             self.params['token'])
    68	                raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    69	
    70	            audience = self.token.id_token.get('aud')
    71	            if not audience:
    72	                logger.debug('[Introspection] No audience found for token: %s',
    73	                             self.params['token'])
    74	                raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    75	
    76	            if audience not in self.client.scope:
    77	                logger.debug('[Introspection] Client %s does not audience scope %s',
    78	                             self.params['client_id'], audience)
    79	                raise TokenIntrospectionError()
    80	
    81	    def create_response_dic(self):
    82	        response_dic = {}
    83	        if self.id_token:
    84	            for k in ('aud', 'sub', 'exp', 'iat', 'iss'):
    85	                response_dic[k] = self.id_token[k]
    86	        response_dic['active'] = True
    87	        response_dic['client_id'] = self.token.client.client_id
    88	
    89	        response_dic = run_processing_hook(response_dic,
    90	                                           'OIDC_INTROSPECTION_PROCESSING_HOOK',
    91	                                           client=self.client,
    92	                                           id_token=self.id_token)
    93	
    94	        return response_dic
    95	
    96	    @classmethod
    97	    def response(cls, dic, status=200):
    98	        """
    99	        Create and return a response object.
   100	        """
   101	        response = JsonResponse(dic, status=status)
   102	        response['Cache-Control'] = 'no-store'
   103	        response['Pragma'] = 'no-cache'
   104	
   105	        return response

   175	def import_from_str(value):
   176	    """
   177	    Attempt to import a class from a string representation.
   178	    """
   179	    try:
   180	        parts = value.split('.')
   181	        module_path, class_name = '.'.join(parts[:-1]), parts[-1]
   182	        module = importlib.import_module(module_path)
   183	        return getattr(module, class_name)
   184	    except ImportError as e:
   185	        msg = 'Could not import %s for settings. %s: %s.' % (value, e.__class__.__name__, e)
   186	        raise ImportError(msg)
   187	
   188	
   189	def get(name, import_str=False):
   190	    """
   191	    Helper function to use inside the package.
   192	    """
   193	    value = None
   194	    default_value = getattr(default_settings, name)
   195	
   196	    try:
   197	        value = getattr(settings, name)
   198	    except AttributeError:
   199	        if name in default_settings.required_attrs:
   200	            raise Exception('You must set ' + name + ' in your settings.')
   201	
   202	    if isinstance(default_value, dict) and value:
   203	        default_value.update(value)
   204	        value = default_value
   205	    else:
   206	        if value is None:
   207	            value = default_value
   208	        value = import_from_str(value) if import_str else value
   209	
   210	    return value
   211

   204	class Token(BaseCodeTokenModel):
   205	
   206	    user = models.ForeignKey(
   207	        settings.AUTH_USER_MODEL, null=True, verbose_name=_(u'User'), on_delete=models.CASCADE)
   208	    access_token = models.CharField(max_length=255, unique=True, verbose_name=_(u'Access Token'))
   209	    refresh_token = models.CharField(max_length=255, unique=True, verbose_name=_(u'Refresh Token'))
   210	    _id_token = models.TextField(verbose_name=_(u'ID Token'))
   211	
   212	    class Meta:
   213	        verbose_name = _(u'Token')
   214	        verbose_name_plural = _(u'Tokens')
   215	
   216	    @property
   217	    def id_token(self):
   218	        return json.loads(self._id_token) if self._id_token else None
   219	
   220	    @id_token.setter
   221	    def id_token(self, value):
   222	        self._id_token = json.dumps(value)
   223	
   224	    def __str__(self):
   225	        return u'{0} - {1}'.format(self.client, self.access_token)
   226	
   227	    @property
   228	    def at_hash(self):
   229	        # @@@ d-o-p only supports 256 bits (change this if that changes)
   230	        hashed_access_token = sha256(
   231	            self.access_token.encode('ascii')
   232	        ).hexdigest().encode('ascii')
   233	        return base64.urlsafe_b64encode(
   234	            binascii.unhexlify(
   235	                hashed_access_token[:len(hashed_access_token) // 2]
   236	            )
   237	        ).rstrip(b'=').decode('ascii')

    22	def create_id_token(token, user, aud, nonce='', at_hash='', request=None, scope=None):
    23	    """
    24	    Creates the id_token dictionary.
    25	    See: http://openid.net/specs/openid-connect-core-1_0.html#IDToken
    26	    Return a dic.
    27	    """
    28	    if scope is None:
    29	        scope = []
    30	    sub = settings.get('OIDC_IDTOKEN_SUB_GENERATOR', import_str=True)(user=user)
    31	
    32	    expires_in = settings.get('OIDC_IDTOKEN_EXPIRE')
    33	
    34	    # Convert datetimes into timestamps.
    35	    now = int(time.time())
    36	    iat_time = now
    37	    exp_time = int(now + expires_in)
    38	    user_auth_time = user.last_login or user.date_joined
    39	    auth_time = int(dateformat.format(user_auth_time, 'U'))
    40	
    41	    dic = {
    42	        'iss': get_issuer(request=request),
    43	        'sub': sub,
    44	        'aud': str(aud),
    45	        'exp': exp_time,
    46	        'iat': iat_time,
    47	        'auth_time': auth_time,
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    if nonce:
    51	        dic['nonce'] = str(nonce)
    52	
    53	    if at_hash:
    54	        dic['at_hash'] = at_hash
    55	
    56	    # Inlude (or not) user standard claims in the id_token.
    57	    if settings.get('OIDC_IDTOKEN_INCLUDE_CLAIMS'):
    58	        standard_claims = StandardScopeClaims(token)
    59	        dic.update(standard_claims.create_response_dic())
    60	        if settings.get('OIDC_EXTRA_SCOPE_CLAIMS'):
    61	            custom_claims = settings.get('OIDC_EXTRA_SCOPE_CLAIMS', import_str=True)(token)
    62	            dic.update(custom_claims.create_response_dic())
    63	
    64	    dic = run_processing_hook(
    65	        dic, 'OIDC_IDTOKEN_PROCESSING_HOOK',
    66	        user=user, token=token, request=request)
    67	
    68	    return dic
    69	
    70	
    71	def encode_id_token(payload, client):
    72	    """
    73	    Represent the ID Token as a JSON Web Token (JWT).
    74	    Return a hash.
    75	    """
    76	    keys = get_client_alg_keys(client)
    77	    _jws = JWS(payload, alg=client.jwt_alg)
    78	    return _jws.sign_compact(keys)
    79	
    80	
    81	def decode_id_token(token, client):
    82	    """
    83	    Represent the ID Token as a JSON Web Token (JWT).
    84	    Return a hash.
    85	    """
    86	    keys = get_client_alg_keys(client)
    87	    return JWS().verify_compact(token, keys=keys)
    88	
    89	
    90	def client_id_from_id_token(id_token):
    91	    """
    92	    Extracts the client id from a JSON Web Token (JWT).
    93	    Returns a string or None.
    94	    """
    95	    payload = JWT().unpack(id_token).payload()
    96	    aud = payload.get('aud', None)
    97	    if aud is None:
    98	        return None
    99	    if isinstance(aud, list):
   100	        return aud[0]
   101	    return aud
   102	
   103	
   104	def create_token(user, client, scope, id_token_dic=None):
   105	    """
   106	    Create and populate a Token object.
   107	    Return a Token object.
   108	    """
   109	    token = Token()
   110	    token.user = user
   111	    token.client = client
   112	    token.access_token = uuid.uuid4().hex
   113	
   114	    if id_token_dic is not None:
   115	        token.id_token = id_token_dic
   116	
   117	    token.refresh_token = uuid.uuid4().hex
   118	    token.expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(
   119	        seconds=settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'))
   120	    token.scope = scope
   121	
   122	    return token
   123	
   124	
   125	def create_code(user, client, scope, nonce, is_authentication,
   126	                code_challenge=None, code_challenge_method=None):
   127	    """
   128	    Create and populate a Code object.
   129	    Return a Code object.
   130	    """
   131	    code = Code()
   132	    code.user = user
   133	    code.client = client
   134	
   135	    code.code = uuid.uuid4().hex
   136	
   137	    if code_challenge and code_challenge_method:
   138	        code.code_challenge = code_challenge
   139	        code.code_challenge_method = code_challenge_method
   140	
   141	    code.expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(
   142	        seconds=settings.get('OIDC_CODE_EXPIRE'))
   143	    code.scope = scope
   144	    code.nonce = nonce
   145	    code.is_authentication = is_authentication
   146	
   147	    return code
   148	
   149	
   150	def get_client_alg_keys(client):
   151	    """
   152	    Takes a client and returns the set of keys associated with it.
   153	    Returns a list of keys.
   154	    """
   155	    if client.jwt_alg == 'RS256':
   156	        keys = []
   157	        for rsakey in RSAKey.objects.all():
   158	            keys.append(jwk_RSAKey(key=importKey(rsakey.key), kid=rsakey.kid))
   159	        if not keys:
   160	            raise Exception('You must add at least one RSA Key.')
   161	    elif client.jwt_alg == 'HS256':
   162	        keys = [SYMKey(key=client.client_secret, alg=client.jwt_alg)]
   163	    else:
   164	        raise Exception('Unsupported key algorithm.')
   165	
   166	    return keys
   167

## Phase 4. TEST CREATION: Creating a Reproduction Script<tool_call>think<arg_key>thought</arg_key><arg_value>Based on my exploration, I now understand the current behavior:

1. In `token.py`, the password grant (`create_access_token_response_dic`) always uses `self.params['scope'].split(' ')` which when empty gives `['']` (single empty string element).

2. The client_credentials grant (`create_client_credentials_response_dic`) correctly uses `self.client.scope` when no scope is provided.

3. There's no scope validation in either case - if invalid scopes are provided, they're just stored.

4. The introspection endpoint doesn't include scope in the response.

Let me create a test script to reproduce the current behavior and then implement the fixes.</arg_value></tool_call><|observation|><tool_response>Your thought has been logged.</tool_response><|assistant|></think><tool_call>str_replace_editor<arg_key>path</arg_key><arg_value>/workspace/juanifioren__django-oidc-provider__0.7/test_reproduction.py</arg_value><arg_key>command</arg_key><arg_value>create</arg_value><arg_key>file_text</arg_key><arg_value>#!/usr/bin/env python
"""
Test script to reproduce the current behavior and verify the fix.
"""
import os
import sys
import django
from django.conf import settings
from django.test.utils import get_runner

# Setup Django
os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'oidc_provider.tests.settings')
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/juanifioren__django-oidc-provider__0.7')

django.setup()

from django.test import TestCase, override_settings
from django.core.management import call_command
import json
from oidc_provider.tests.app.utils import create_fake_user, create_fake_client
from oidc_provider.models import Token


def test_current_behavior():
    """Test the current behavior to understand the issues."""
    print("=== Testing Current Behavior ===")
    
    # Setup
    call_command('creatersakey')
    user = create_fake_user()
    client = create_fake_client(response_type='code')
    
    # Add some scopes to the client
    client._scope = 'openid email profile'
    client.save()
    
    print(f"Client scopes: {client.scope}")
    
    # Test 1: Password grant without scope parameter
    print("\n--- Test 1: Password grant WITHOUT scope parameter ---")
    from django.test import RequestFactory
    from oidc_provider.lib.endpoints.token import TokenEndpoint
    
    factory = RequestFactory()
    
    # Simulate password grant without scope
    post_data = {
        'grant_type': 'password',
        'username': 'johndoe',
        'password': '1234',
        'client_id': client.client_id,
        'client_secret': client.client_secret,
    }
    
    request = factory.post('/token', data=post_data)
    endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
    
    try:
        with override_settings(OIDC_GRANT_TYPE_PASSWORD_ENABLE=True):
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            # Get the created token
            token = Token.objects.get(access_token=response_dict['access_token'])
            print(f"Token scopes: {token.scope}")
            print(f"Expected: {client.scope}")
            print(f"Issue: Token has empty scope instead of client scopes")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error: {e}")
        
    # Test 2: Password grant with scope parameter
    print("\n--- Test 2: Password grant WITH scope parameter ---")
    post_data_with_scope = {
        'grant_type': 'password',
        'username': 'johndoe',
        'password': '1234',
        'client_id': client.client_id,
        'client_secret': client.client_secret,
        'scope': 'openid email'
    }
    
    request = factory.post('/token', data=post_data_with_scope)
    endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
    
    try:
        with override_settings(OIDC_GRANT_TYPE_PASSWORD_ENABLE=True):
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            # Get the created token
            token = Token.objects.get(access_token=response_dict['access_token'])
            print(f"Token scopes: {token.scope}")
            print(f"Requested scopes: ['openid', 'email']")
            print(f"This should work correctly")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error: {e}")
        
    # Test 3: Client credentials without scope parameter (current behavior)
    print("\n--- Test 3: Client credentials grant ---")
    post_data_cc = {
        'grant_type': 'client_credentials',
        'client_id': client.client_id,
        'client_secret': client.client_secret,
    }
    
    request = factory.post('/token', data=post_data_cc)
    endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
    
    try:
        endpoint.validate_params()
        response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
        
        # Get the created token
        token = Token.objects.get(access_token=response_dict['access_token'])
        print(f"Token scopes: {token.scope}")
        print(f"Client scopes: {client.scope}")
        print(f"This should work correctly (uses client scopes)")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error: {e}")

"""
Test script to reproduce the current behavior and verify the fix.
"""

from django.test import TestCase, override_settings
from django.core.management import call_command
from django.test import RequestFactory
import json
from oidc_provider.tests.app.utils import create_fake_user, create_fake_client
from oidc_provider.models import Token
from oidc_provider.lib.endpoints.token import TokenEndpoint


class ScopeBehaviorTest(TestCase):
    """Test the current behavior to understand the issues."""

    def setUp(self):
        call_command('creatersakey')
        self.factory = RequestFactory()
        self.user = create_fake_user()
        self.client = create_fake_client(response_type='code')
        
        # Add some scopes to the client
        self.client._scope = 'openid email profile'
        self.client.save()
        
        print(f"Client scopes: {self.client.scope}")

    @override_settings(OIDC_GRANT_TYPE_PASSWORD_ENABLE=True)
    def test_password_grant_without_scope(self):
        """Test password grant without scope parameter."""
        print("\n--- Test 1: Password grant WITHOUT scope parameter ---")
        
        # Simulate password grant without scope
        post_data = {
            'grant_type': 'password',
            'username': 'johndoe',
            'password': '1234',
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/token', data=post_data)
        endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            # Get the created token
            token = Token.objects.get(access_token=response_dict['access_token'])
            print(f"Token scopes: {token.scope}")
            print(f"Expected: {self.client.scope}")
            print(f"Issue: Token has empty/unexpected scope instead of client scopes")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error: {e}")

    @override_settings(OIDC_GRANT_TYPE_PASSWORD_ENABLE=True)
    def test_password_grant_with_scope(self):
        """Test password grant with scope parameter."""
        print("\n--- Test 2: Password grant WITH scope parameter ---")
        
        post_data_with_scope = {
            'grant_type': 'password',
            'username': 'johndoe',
            'password': '1234',
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
            'scope': 'openid email'
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/token', data=post_data_with_scope)
        endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            # Get the created token
            token = Token.objects.get(access_token=response_dict['access_token'])
            print(f"Token scopes: {token.scope}")
            print(f"Requested scopes: ['openid', 'email']")
            print(f"This should work correctly")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error: {e}")

    def test_client_credentials_grant(self):
        """Test client credentials grant."""
        print("\n--- Test 3: Client credentials grant ---")
        
        post_data_cc = {
            'grant_type': 'client_credentials',
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/token', data=post_data_cc)
        endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            # Get the created token
            token = Token.objects.get(access_token=response_dict['access_token'])
            print(f"Token scopes: {token.scope}")
            print(f"Client scopes: {self.client.scope}")
            print(f"This should work correctly (uses client scopes)")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error: {e}")

    @override_settings(OIDC_GRANT_TYPE_PASSWORD_ENABLE=True)
    def test_password_grant_with_invalid_scope(self):
        """Test password grant with invalid scope."""
        print("\n--- Test 4: Password grant with INVALID scope ---")
        
        post_data_with_invalid_scope = {
            'grant_type': 'password',
            'username': 'johndoe',
            'password': '1234',
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
            'scope': 'invalid_scope'
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/token', data=post_data_with_invalid_scope)
        endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            print(f"ERROR: Should have failed with invalid_scope but didn't!")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Correctly caught error: {e}")

    def test_client_credentials_with_empty_scope(self):
        """Test client credentials grant with empty client scope."""
        print("\n--- Test 5: Client credentials with EMPTY client scope ---")
        
        # Remove scopes from client
        self.client._scope = ''
        self.client.save()
        
        post_data_cc = {
            'grant_type': 'client_credentials',
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/token', data=post_data_cc)
        endpoint = TokenEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            print(f"ERROR: Should have failed with invalid_scope but didn't!")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Correctly caught error: {e}")

   185	
   186	    def create_refresh_response_dic(self):
   187	        # See https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-6
   188	
   189	        scope_param = self.params['scope']
   190	        scope = (scope_param.split(' ') if scope_param else self.token.scope)
   191	        unauthorized_scopes = set(scope) - set(self.token.scope)
   192	        if unauthorized_scopes:
   193	            raise TokenError('invalid_scope')
   194	
   195	        token = create_token(
   196	            user=self.token.user,
   197	            client=self.token.client,
   198	            scope=scope)
   199	
   200	        # If the Token has an id_token it's an Authentication request.
   201	        if self.token.id_token:
   202	            id_token_dic = create_id_token(
   203	                user=self.token.user,
   204	                aud=self.client.client_id,
   205	                token=token,
   206	                nonce=None,
   207	                at_hash=token.at_hash,
   208	                request=self.request,
   209	                scope=token.scope,
   210	            )
   211	        else:
   212	            id_token_dic = {}
   213	        token.id_token = id_token_dic
   214	
   215	        # Store the token.
   216	        token.save()
   217	
   218	        # Forget the old token.
   219	        self.token.delete()
   220	
   221	        dic = {
   222	            'access_token': token.access_token,
   223	            'refresh_token': token.refresh_token,
   224	            'token_type': 'bearer',
   225	            'expires_in': settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'),
   226	            'id_token': encode_id_token(id_token_dic, self.token.client),
   227	        }
   228	
   229	        return dic

        # See https://tools.ietf.org/html/rfc6749#section-4.3

        # Handle scope parameter for password grant
        scope_param = self.params['scope']
        # If no scope provided, use client's scope
        if not scope_param:
            scope = self.client.scope
        else:
            scope = scope_param.split(' ')
            
        # Validate that requested scopes are subset of client's allowed scopes
        unauthorized_scopes = set(scope) - set(self.client.scope)
        if unauthorized_scopes:
            raise TokenError('invalid_scope')

        token = create_token(
            self.user,
            self.client,
            scope)

        id_token_dic = create_id_token(
            token=token,
            user=self.user,
            aud=self.client.client_id,
            nonce='self.code.nonce',
            at_hash=token.at_hash,
            request=self.request,
            scope=token.scope,
        )

        token.id_token = id_token_dic
        token.save()

        return {
            'access_token': token.access_token,
            'refresh_token': token.refresh_token,
            'expires_in': settings.get('OIDC_TOKEN_EXPIRE'),
            'token_type': 'bearer',

    def OIDC_INTROSPECTION_VALIDATE_AUDIENCE_SCOPE(self):
        """
        OPTIONAL: A boolean to specify whether or not to verify that the introspection
        resource has the requesting client id as one of its scopes.
        """
        return True

    @property
    def OIDC_INTROSPECTION_RESPONSE_SCOPE_ENABLE(self):
        """
        OPTIONAL: A boolean to specify whether or not to include the 'scope' field

        }

    def test_introspection_with_scope_enabled(self):
        """Test introspection with scope in response."""
        print("\n--- Test 8: Introspection with scope ENABLED ---")
        
        # Add introspection scope to client
        from oidc_provider.lib.endpoints.introspection import INTROSPECTION_SCOPE
        self.client._scope = 'openid email profile ' + INTROSPECTION_SCOPE
        self.client.save()
        
        # Create a token first
        from oidc_provider.lib.utils.token import create_token
        token = create_token(self.user, self.client, ['openid', 'email'])
        token.save()
        
        from oidc_provider.lib.endpoints.introspection import TokenIntrospectionEndpoint
        
        post_data = {
            'token': token.access_token,
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/introspect', data=post_data)
        endpoint = TokenIntrospectionEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            if 'scope' in response_dict:
                expected_scope = 'openid email'
                actual_scope = response_dict['scope']
                if actual_scope == expected_scope:
                    print(f"SUCCESS: Scope correctly included: '{actual_scope}'")
                else:
                    print(f"ERROR: Wrong scope value. Expected '{expected_scope}', got '{actual_scope}'")
            else:
                print(f"ERROR: Scope should be in response when enabled")
                
        except Exception as e:

    def test_introspection_with_scope_enabled(self):
        """Test introspection with scope in response."""
        print("\n--- Test 8: Introspection with scope ENABLED ---")
        
        # Add introspection scope to client
        from oidc_provider.lib.endpoints.introspection import INTROSPECTION_SCOPE
        self.client._scope = 'openid email profile ' + INTROSPECTION_SCOPE
        self.client.save()
        
        # Create a token first
        from oidc_provider.lib.utils.token import create_token
        token = create_token(self.user, self.client, ['openid', 'email'])
        token.save()
        
        from oidc_provider.lib.endpoints.introspection import TokenIntrospectionEndpoint
        
        post_data = {
            'token': token.access_token,
            'client_id': self.client.client_id,
            'client_secret': self.client.client_secret,
        }
        
        request = self.factory.post('/introspect', data=post_data)
        endpoint = TokenIntrospectionEndpoint(request)
        
        try:
            endpoint.validate_params()
            response_dict = endpoint.create_response_dic()
            
            if 'scope' in response_dict:
                expected_scope = 'openid email'
                actual_scope = response_dict['scope']
                if actual_scope == expected_scope:
                    print(f"SUCCESS: Scope correctly included: '{actual_scope}'")
                else:
                    print(f"ERROR: Wrong scope value. Expected '{expected_scope}', got '{actual_scope}'")
            else:
                print(f"ERROR: Scope should be in response when enabled")
                
        except Exception as e:
